# CortexEngine Final-Art Completion Ledger

Audit date: 2026-05-14

Scope: this ledger converts the Programmatic Final Art Rendering research plan into verifiable CortexEngine implementation requirements and records the current implementation evidence.

This ledger is intentionally stricter than a renderer-feature checklist. Existing renderer foundations can be `DONE_VERIFIED`, but the new final-art plan is complete only when CortexEngine can generate, validate, and admit art-directed scene variants for the first scene family set.

## Completion Gate

Exact completion gate: every item in this ledger must be `DONE_VERIFIED` or `DEFERRED_BY_USER_ONLY`; every `DEFERRED_BY_USER_ONLY` item must have an explicit user statement; no item may remain `PARTIAL`, `NOT_STARTED`, `DONE_UNVERIFIED`, or `BLOCKED`; the worktree must be clean after the final commit; and both technical release validation and final-art validation artifacts must pass from the committed state.

Required final proof:

```powershell
git status --short --ignore-submodules=all
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File CortexEngine/tools/run_release_validation.ps1
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File CortexEngine/tools/run_asset_led_scene_contract_tests.ps1 -NoBuild
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File CortexEngine/tools/run_scene_layering_contract_tests.ps1 -NoBuild
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File CortexEngine/tools/run_scene_transaction_validation.ps1 -NoBuild
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File CortexEngine/tools/run_generated_asset_admission_tests.ps1 -NoBuild
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File CortexEngine/tools/run_final_art_kernel_contract_tests.ps1 -NoBuild
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File CortexEngine/tools/run_final_art_variant_tournament_tests.ps1 -NoBuild
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File CortexEngine/tools/run_final_art_visual_validation.ps1 -NoBuild
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File CortexEngine/tools/run_final_art_contact_sheet.ps1 -NoBuild
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File CortexEngine/tools/run_final_art_pretrained_asset_plan_tests.ps1 -NoBuild
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File CortexEngine/tools/run_final_art_pretrained_adapter_job_tests.ps1 -NoBuild
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File CortexEngine/tools/run_final_art_pretrained_asset_import_contract_tests.ps1 -NoBuild
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File CortexEngine/tools/run_final_art_pretrained_scene_assembly_tests.ps1 -NoBuild
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File CortexEngine/tools/run_final_art_pretrained_visual_rejection_tests.ps1 -NoBuild
```

The final-art commands now exist and pass as focused validation. Full release validation also passes after hardening the visual-probe runtime budget harness against transient one-frame GPU timing spikes without weakening the budget threshold. Pretrained-asset request, provider-adapter job export, import, assembly, visual-rejection contracts, local Shap-E generation, real glTF mesh conversion, Cortex runtime loading, focused capture, and image-scored promotion now exist for the first rain-tabletop candidate.

## Highest-Priority Incomplete Items

`L001` remains `PARTIAL`. The original final-art V0 and first pretrained runtime-promotion requirements are `DONE_VERIFIED`, but the active human-level model-authored art goal is broader and still incomplete.

## Items Most Likely To Cause Premature Closure

- `CE-FA-030`: this was the premature-closure risk because focused final-art validation passed before full release validation did. It is now verified by full release validation, but future changes must keep the full release gate as the close condition.
- `CE-FA-017` to `CE-FA-019`: these have generated candidate artifacts and rendered hero captures, but future quality work should still improve the screenshots; do not reinterpret this V0 validation as the end of all artistic polish.
- `CE-FA-042`: this premature-closure risk is now verified for the first rain-tabletop candidate by actual imported meshes, Cortex runtime capture, image scoring, and a promoted image artifact. Do not generalize that proof to every future scene family without adding new ledger items.
- Larger authored content, broader game-content production, and future scene-family expansion must not be counted as SOTA architecture completion unless this ledger explicitly tracks the implementation and validation evidence for that content.

### L001 Scene Authoring Intelligence V1 Best-Per-Scene Addendum - 2026-05-24

1. Requirement ID: L001
2. Requirement text: Human-level final art taste is solved.
3. Current status: PARTIAL, NOT COMPLETE.
4. What changed: The full pipeline now writes a merged best-per-scene visual audit so partial one-scene branches no longer replace the whole batch. Semantic-detail grouping consumes the merged audit and selected all three scenes in the office/gym/classroom probe. The semantic critic now identifies basketball rims by role/category/assembly instead of prompt words in generated IDs, preventing floor-line detail meshes from blocking gym cleanup as fake hoop rims. The final merged audit is updated after positive-taste repair.

### L001 2026-05-24 Positive-Taste Repair Addendum

1. Requirement ID: L001
2. Current status: PARTIAL
3. New evidence: `tools/run_scene_authoring_positive_taste_repair_batch.py` adds a reusable audit-driven repair stage for weak positive-taste views. It uses view-local color contrast and support-bound mid-frequency detail operators, validates candidates before rendering, and relies on rendered admission plus integrity/visual-audit gates before promotion. `tools/run_scene_authoring_full_pipeline.py` now exposes this as `--run-positive-taste-repair-loop`.
4. Related source changes: `tools/run_scene_authoring_positive_taste_repair_batch.py`; `tools/run_scene_authoring_full_pipeline.py`; `tools/scene_graph_kernel_v1.py`.
5. SGK correctness fix: role-token category inference now matches on alphanumeric boundaries, preventing false positives such as `cabinet_detail` being classified as `basketball_net` while preserving matches such as `stove_mesh` -> `stove`.
6. Exact proof: `python -m py_compile tools\run_scene_authoring_full_pipeline.py tools\scene_graph_kernel_v1.py tools\run_scene_authoring_positive_taste_repair_batch.py`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_scene_graph_kernel_v1_tests.ps1`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_scene_authoring_full_pipeline_tests.ps1`; v8e positive-taste repair, rendered admission, promotion, integrity gate, visual-scene audit, and full-pipeline optional-loop commands recorded in `docs/SCENE_GRAPH_KERNEL_V1_LIVING_LEDGER.md`.

Latest evidence: v8e tight-budget kitchen candidate selected `docs/media/final_art/generated/model_authored/model_authored_multiview_stress_rainy_galley_kitchen_scene_authoring_kernel_v1_semantic_detail_group_probe_f_ff8bb3953ed5.json`, improving min positive taste `0.833190 -> 0.862334` and mean positive taste `0.851877 -> 0.889603`, with zero rendered defects. Integrity gate passed with `blocking_check_count=0` at `artifacts/scene_authoring_kernel_v1/semantic_detail_group_probe_from_v17_selected_v8e_positive_taste_tight_budget_integrity/integrity_gate_batch_report.json`. Visual scene audit passed with `issue_count=0` at `artifacts/scene_authoring_kernel_v1/semantic_detail_group_probe_from_v17_selected_v8e_positive_taste_tight_budget_visual_audit/visual_scene_audit_batch_report.json`. 6. What currently exists: The constructor cascade now preserves repeated admitted stadium seat meshes instead of deleting them as duplicate physical claims. `model_scene_physical_minutia_repair.py` terraces stadium bleacher rows, creates a concrete `phys_stadium_bleacher_deck` support, keeps row/seat support relations explicit, and preserves the original 15 admitted Kenney seat assets through the real stadium cascade. `model_scene_visual_admission_preservation_gate.py` is integrated into `model_scene_constructor_cascade.py` as a render-eligibility gate so constructor stages cannot silently remove admitted runtime assets. The v355 stadium candidate passes constructor preflight, visual-admission preservation, and six-view rendered multiview composition after the earlier v352 failure. For the neon concert family, `model_scene_visual_role_compiler.py` is now integrated into `model_scene_constructor_cascade.py`; the cascade emits a `visual_role_compilation` stage that adds screen/performer/rig/detail/support-aware roles without losing the protected stage layout. `model_scene_bounded_final_art_feedback_loop.py` now wraps the proven rendered-feedback sequence into a reusable bounded loop: focal hierarchy from art-critic evidence, view-metric camera restage variants, no-regression tournament selection, and narrow existing-light colorfulness tuning. The office family now has an integrated scene-kit enrichment pass with bookcase rows, supported labels, cork-board notes, grounded floor storage, desk equipment, and support-overlap repair for generated desk papers. The kitchen family now has integrated support-aware counter/sink detail enrichment and a late physical-minutia repair pass for small surface props added after earlier family/detail stages.
7. What is missing: This is not human-level final art. v374 proves a concert slice can clear the current automated five-view rendered gate, but that is not proof that human-level taste is solved. The stadium still has asset-fidelity debt, primitive bleachers/floodlights/goals, and only automated review. The full constructor-feedback loop still needs to be generalized and proven across kitchen, classroom, gym, concert, office, red room, stadium, and future families; it must also handle richer mesh generation/fidelity and human-selected taste memories rather than only satisfying current art-critic heuristics.
8. Validation required: The scene-authoring intelligence layer must fuse constructor debt, relation gates, rendered evidence, human/taste memory, and optional pretrained/VLM-style critic proposals into ranked operator plans without directly mutating scenes; the authoring controller must convert those plans into concrete evidence/model/repair/render/admission work orders, pass rendered images and view manifests into model critique packets, and keep neural proposals behind ingestor admission; focused repair contract must preserve repeated admitted seat meshes; position-awareness repair must snap/nudge grounded objects and move `attached_to` descendants with repaired supports; imported/open-source mesh roles must be admitted through the same support, attachment, and collision checks as primitive blockouts; late family/visual-role additions must receive scene-family-aware assembly, slot, or attachment metadata before final admission; late support-child slot compilation must give support-only visible children object-sized slots before late relation coverage; scene-local environment compilation and constructor debt must reject or repair enclosed-scene IBL/background/reflection leakage and require seed-authored local lights before render; visual preservation gate must fail a known-bad asset-loss fixture and pass a preserved fixture; constructor cascade must keep all admitted assets, run final position-awareness, late physical-minutia repair, late support-child slot compilation, late relation-coverage compilation, and scene-local environment compilation after late visual/family additions, and pass preflight; six-view stadium render must pass composition; visual-role compiler must add contract-covered screen/performer/rig/detail roles while passing composition and relation pre-render gates; focal-hierarchy compiler must pass its regression test and materially improve or clear rendered art-critic defects; camera restage grid and selector must choose a no-regression rendered variant; bounded concert colorfulness tuning must modify only existing lights and not add unsafe geometry; bounded final-art feedback loop must stage focal/restage/selection/colorfulness actions and emit required render commands; office scene-kit enrichment must pass support-contact/relation gates and rendered multiview; kitchen counter/sink detail enrichment must reject unsupported or footprint-invalid details, pass constructor gates, and clear rendered kitchen multiview through bounded restage when geometry alone is insufficient; broader family cascade tests must remain green after constructor changes.

Additional 2026-05-17 imported-mesh physical admission evidence: `tools/model_scene_physical_minutia_validator.py` now treats common imported/open-source mesh roles as first-class physical objects instead of letting them bypass primitive-focused checks. Floor-standing mesh classes such as `classroom_chair_mesh`, `classroom_student_desk_mesh`, `auditorium_seat_mesh`, `foreground_audience_chair_mesh`, `sideline_equipment_mesh`, `sofa_mesh`, `speaker_stack_mesh`, and `venue_silhouette_prop_mesh` must satisfy support/contact and collision checks. Attached mesh classes such as `wall_light_mesh`, `ceiling_light_mesh`, `overhead_light_mesh`, `stage_light_fixture_mesh`, `scoreboard_mesh`, `range_hood`, `wall_art_mesh`, and `backboard_mount_mesh` must attach to their declared surface, with explicit bounded allowances for suspended fixtures so real ceiling fans and concert lights are not misclassified as floating. The focused contract now includes a bad imported chair mesh floating above the floor and a detached imported wall light, both of which must fail, plus corresponding good mesh fixtures that must pass. Validation passed: `python -m py_compile tools\model_scene_physical_minutia_validator.py`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_physical_minutia_validator_tests.ps1` reported `PASS bad_failures=9 good_checks=5 bad_gym_failures=10 good_gym_checks=10`; and `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_constructor_cascade_tests.ps1 -FamilyMatrix` passed all 7 families under the stricter mesh-admission rules with final seeds at `artifacts/constructor_cascade_tests/cascade_*/07yg_constructor_ready_scene_seed.json`. This closes the ordering gap where family-specific enrichers and visual-role passes could add visible objects after the earlier relation-coverage compiler had already run. `tools/model_scene_constructor_relation_coverage_gate.py` now treats shelves, storage, fixtures, screens, and lights as significant constructor objects rather than letting support structures remain transform-only. `tools/model_scene_constructor_relation_coverage_compiler.py` now has scene-family-aware assembly inference for office, classroom, gym, concert, and stadium objects; it uses token matching for short terms so `rim` does not accidentally match words like `primitive`. Additional 2026-05-17 scene-local environment evidence: `tools/model_scene_constructor_debt_report.py` now treats static environment-contract failures as blocking `material_lighting_balance` constructor debt, so enclosed scenes with HDRI/IBL background exposure, environment reflections, non-neutral ambient source, missing no-HDRI validation flags, or fewer than two seed-authored local lights cannot enter render admission as if they were final-art candidates. `tools/model_scene_scene_local_environment_compiler.py` now runs inside `tools/model_scene_constructor_cascade.py` as `scene_local_environment_compilation` after late relation coverage and before visual preservation, constructor debt, relation pre-render, and preflight. This preserves already-compliant authored family seeds and repairs malformed/generated fixtures by writing `neutral_procedural`, zero IBL diffuse/specular intensity, disabled HDRI background/reflections, authored-geometry-only background policy, scene-authored ambient source, no-HDRI validation flags, enclosure flags, and deterministic local key/fill lights when needed. Post-matrix `model_scene_environment_contract_validator.py` checks passed for the final family seeds. Additional 2026-05-17 scene-authoring intelligence evidence: `tools/model_scene_authoring_intelligence.py` adds the first explicit authoring-brain layer above the constructor and rendered-feedback tools. It builds a blackboard from constructor/debt/relation/rendered/human/taste/model-critic evidence, scores routes, emits a ranked operator plan, and records a neural-model boundary: pretrained/VLM models may propose through JSON packets and ingestor admission, but direct scene mutation is forbidden and deterministic fallback remains required. `tools/model_scene_authoring_controller.py` now converts a brain plan into concrete work orders for evidence acquisition, model packet export, external/deterministic model proposal admission, constructor repair, rendered policy search, camera restage, and re-planning. The controller extracts rendered image paths and view-manifest directories from multiview reports so a model can actually inspect the scene while remaining unable to mutate it directly. The model proposal ingestor now admits the action vocabulary the brain already routes (`attach_to_parent`, `compile_relation`, `instantiate_asset`, `upgrade_asset`, `material_intent_cluster`, `adjust_light`) while still requiring known graph ids, known relation types, no completion claims, and deterministic validation. Focused validation passed: `python -m py_compile tools\model_scene_authoring_controller.py tools\model_scene_authoring_intelligence.py tools\model_scene_model_proposal_ingestor.py tools\model_scene_pretrained_critic_runner.py tools\model_scene_critique_packet_exporter.py`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_authoring_controller_tests.ps1`, covering constructor-repair work orders, evidence-acquisition work orders, rendered-policy work orders, external model proposal admission commands, and rendered-image/view-manifest propagation; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_authoring_intelligence_tests.ps1`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_pretrained_critic_runner_tests.ps1`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_critique_packet_tests.ps1`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_diagnostic_proposal_generator_tests.ps1`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_taste_memory_ingestor_material_tests.ps1`; and `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_relation_proposal_runner_tests.ps1`. Real kitchen controller evidence: `python tools\model_scene_authoring_controller.py --seed artifacts\constructor_cascade_tests\cascade_home_kitchen_lantern\07yi_constructor_ready_scene_seed.json --constructor-debt-report artifacts\constructor_cascade_tests\cascade_home_kitchen_lantern\07zz_constructor_debt_report.json --relation-report artifacts\constructor_cascade_tests\cascade_home_kitchen_lantern\08_relation_prerender_gate_report.json --multiview-report docs\media\final_art\generated\model_authored\model_authored_multiview_home_kitchen_v402_wider_left_integrated_restage.json --model-critic-report artifacts\constructor_cascade_tests\cascade_home_kitchen_lantern\pretrained_critic_authoring_probe.json --out-root artifacts\scene_authoring_controller\home_kitchen_v402 --out artifacts\scene_authoring_controller\home_kitchen_v402\work_order.json` produced a `SCENE_AUTHORING_CONTROLLER_WORK_ORDER_READY` evidence-acquisition work order with six rendered images in the model request. Running the generated model-critic path with those six images and diagnostic reports produced `MODEL_CRITIC_RUNNER_READY` with three admitted proposals at `artifacts\scene_authoring_controller\home_kitchen_v402\home_kitchen_lantern_home_kitchen_lantern_positional_relation_constructor_repair_support_child_slots\model_critic_report.json`; replanning with that report changed the brain to `REPAIR_CONSTRUCTOR_BEFORE_RENDER`, primary route `support_contact`, and the subsequent controller work order `artifacts\scene_authoring_controller\home_kitchen_v402_replanned\work_order.json` contains a constructor-cascade repair command before any promotion. Additional 2026-05-17 cross-family hot-mass policy evidence: `tools/model_scene_authoring_intelligence.py` now separates `hot_mass_views` from ordinary focal drift and routes central blown-out/hot-mass failures to `material_lighting_balance`, weakening camera-only repair when overbright screen-space pressure is present. `tools/model_scene_screen_space_hot_mass_policy.py` was added as a reusable authoring-brain operator: it reads a rendered multiview report, finds hot-mass views, runs screen-space admission on the exact validation view seeds, and emits a bounded material-dampened candidate seed behind normal relation/preflight/rendered admission. `tools/model_scene_authoring_controller.py` now emits a `run_screen_space_hot_mass_policy` work-order command for hot-mass material/lighting routes. That rejection led to a broader screen-space surface policy: `tools/model_scene_stadium_screen_space_color_grade.py` on the same v403 seed improved `midfield_low` to `0.659` and reduced two defects to one, selected as `PARTIAL_PROGRESS_SELECTED` at `artifacts\scene_authoring_controller\stadium_v422_broad_surface_hot_mass\v422_from_v403_selector.json`. Additional 2026-05-17 policy-control evidence: `tools/model_scene_positive_exemplar_policy_loop.py` now reads human review memory inside policy outcome memory. A human `REJECTED_NEEDS_WORK` decision suppresses the selected positive-exemplar policy even if the automated selector admitted it; a human `ACCEPTED_AS_POSITIVE_EXEMPLAR` decision keeps the policy eligible even if older automated outcome memory contained rejection pressure. This makes the authoring controller subordinate to explicit taste feedback instead of scalar automated image scores. Additional 2026-05-17 authoring-brain evidence: `tools/model_scene_authoring_intelligence.py` now understands `positive_exemplar_human_review_memory` directly. Human-accepted positive exemplar records add route evidence for their selected policy family, while human-rejected records penalize the rejected policy and add stronger repair pressure from the recorded defect type, such as `support_contact` for floating/contact failures or `material_lighting_balance`/`semantic_geometry` for IBL/background leakage. This means the next authoring plan can choose constructor repair over another cosmetic variant when human feedback identifies spatial failure. Focused validation passed: `python -m py_compile tools\model_scene_authoring_intelligence.py`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_authoring_intelligence_tests.ps1`, including a fixture where a rejected human support/contact defect becomes the primary route while an accepted exemplar still contributes positive detail-density route memory.
Additional 2026-05-17 human-feedback repair compiler evidence: `tools/model_scene_human_feedback_repair_work_order.py` now converts human art-review defects into executable repair queues instead of leaving them as prose. It maps contact/floating defects to position-awareness and physical-minutia repair, IBL/background leakage to material sanitization and scene-local environment compilation, placeholder quality to asset-fidelity audit, and arrangement/material issues to relation and multiview validation. `tools/model_scene_material_texture_sanitizer.py` was added to strip inherited `assets/textures/rtshowcase/*` texture references from scene-local authored scenes before asset/material review; this directly targets the IBL/showcase bleed that made enclosed scenes read as props in an old environment. `tools/model_scene_authoring_controller.py` now emits `compile_human_feedback_repair_work_order` whenever human feedback or `positive_exemplar_human_review_memory` is present. Additional 2026-05-17 scene-authoring intelligence control-plane evidence: the active goal has been pivoted away from further micro-polish and toward the reusable brain that chooses and composes the existing tools. `tools/model_scene_authoring_intelligence.py` now ingests spatial consistency, semantic geometry, and position-awareness reports as a causal scene blackboard. Additional 2026-05-17 closed model-guided brain-loop evidence: `tools/model_scene_model_guided_repair_loop.py` now provides the reusable architectural piece missing from the earlier toolchain. It builds the model critique packet, accepts external pretrained/VLM-style proposals or deterministic fallback proposals, validates them through `model_scene_model_proposal_ingestor.py`, executes only bounded deterministic variants through `model_scene_relation_proposal_executor.py`, gates each variant with spatial, family-constructor, relation pre-render, and constructor-preflight checks, and emits exact render/selector commands. It also converts rendered multiview weak-detail and hot-mass defects into local fallback proposals when no external model JSON is available, while preserving the no-direct-mutation contract. `tools/model_scene_authoring_controller.py` now emits `run_model_guided_repair_loop` work-order commands with rendered image/view-manifest context, diagnostics, outcome memory, and baseline reports, so model-guided scene repair is part of the normal control plane instead of a manual side path. Focused validation passed: `python -m py_compile tools\model_scene_model_guided_repair_loop.py tools\model_scene_authoring_controller.py`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_model_guided_repair_loop_tests.ps1`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_authoring_controller_tests.ps1`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_authoring_intelligence_tests.ps1`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_pretrained_critic_runner_tests.ps1`; and `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_relation_proposal_executor_tests.ps1`. Real rain run `artifacts\scene_authoring_controller\scene_authoring_intelligence_brain_pivot\rain_model_guided_repair_loop\model_guided_repair_loop.json` admitted 18 proposals, executed 2 render-eligible variants from rendered-defect fallback, and produced render commands for visibility emphasis and lantern material/light balance. Rendering both candidates showed why the brain must learn from rejection: `model_authored_multiview_candidate_16_visibility_emphasis_visibility_emphasis.json` was neutral with no defect reduction, while `model_authored_multiview_candidate_17_material_light_balance_artdir_lantern_hero_visible_warm_core.json` regressed protected/clean views. Selector `rain_model_guided_repair_loop\rendered_progress_selector.json` returned `NO_PROGRESS_SELECTED`; `model_scene_model_proposal_outcome_memory.py` recorded those exact rejected signatures in `model_proposal_outcome_memory_update.json` so future model proposals avoid repeating them. This is real progress toward a scene-authoring brain that can see, propose, manipulate, validate, and remember, but it is not final art completion.

Additional 2026-05-17 dedicated restage brain-loop evidence: the closed model-guided loop now executes dedicated `restage_camera` proposals instead of stopping at `requires dedicated policy operator`. `tools/model_scene_model_guided_repair_loop.py` imports the view-metric restage grid, extracts view-local restage requests from admitted model proposals, generates camera variants, gates each variant with the same spatial/constructor/relation/preflight checks as geometry/material variants, and includes the resulting render commands in the loop report. The rendered-defect fallback now keeps hot-mass restage proposals distinct per view instead of deduplicating them by target object, which previously caused rain to restage `left_three_quarter` while silently ignoring `right_three_quarter`. Focused validation passed: `python -m py_compile tools\model_scene_model_guided_repair_loop.py tools\model_scene_authoring_controller.py`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_model_guided_repair_loop_tests.ps1`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_authoring_controller_tests.ps1`; and `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_authoring_intelligence_tests.ps1`. Real rain v2 loop proved outcome memory worked: the previously rejected material/detail signatures were blocked and the loop emitted seven render-eligible camera-restage variants. Real rain v3 loop proved the per-view dedupe fix: `rain_model_guided_repair_loop_v3\camera_restage_variants\view_metric_restage_grid_report.json` targeted both `left_three_quarter` and `right_three_quarter`. Rendering all seven v3 restage variants produced unique reports `model_authored_multiview_v3_camera_restage_*.json`; several branches improved min/mean score and cleared one hot-mass defect, but selector `rain_model_guided_repair_loop_v3\rendered_progress_selector.json` still returned `NO_PROGRESS_SELECTED` because defect count did not improve and palette/detail defects remained or increased. This is useful negative evidence: camera restage is now a first-class brain operator, but final art requires a composed restage-plus-palette/detail continuation rather than camera-only admission.

Additional 2026-05-17 scene-authoring intelligence strategy evidence: `tools/model_scene_authoring_intelligence.py` now distinguishes ordinary failure from a `NO_PROGRESS_SELECTED` positive near-miss. It summarizes selector decisions, counts repeated rejection causes such as `hot_mass_or_focal_pressure`, `defect_count_not_reduced`, and `protected_view_regression`, chooses the best rejected near-miss by rendered metric deltas, and emits an explicit `strategy_plan` above route ranking. The strategy plan preserves the neural/pretrained model boundary: model/VLM/3D-scene advisors can receive critique packets and emit typed proposals, but cannot mutate scene seeds or claim completion. Focused validation passed: `python -m py_compile tools\model_scene_authoring_intelligence.py`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_authoring_strategy_tests.ps1`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_authoring_intelligence_tests.ps1`; and `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_authoring_controller_tests.ps1`. Executing the first stage with `model_scene_screen_space_hot_mass_policy.py` produced five bounded material changes and passed spatial, relation pre-render, and constructor preflight gates, but rendered admission rejected `model_authored_multiview_wider_left_hero_sst_cyan_hot_mass_followup.json`: it still had the left hot-mass defect and introduced extra palette/hot-mass defects in other views. This is progress in the brain/control-plane architecture and negative evidence for the current dampener policy; it is not final art.

Additional 2026-05-17 policy-sequence memory evidence: `tools/model_scene_policy_sequence_outcome_memory.py` now records rendered outcomes for composed authoring sequences, not just individual model relation proposals or positive-exemplar policies. Additional 2026-05-17 threshold-escalation and near-miss evidence: `tools/model_scene_view_local_defect_composer.py` now reads art-director metrics (`colorfulness`, `edge_density`, and `center_hot_ratio`) from multiview critique reports and emits threshold-specific variants: palette escalation, side-edge density escalation, center-hot control, and combined threshold escalation. Focused validation passed: `python -m py_compile tools\model_scene_view_local_defect_composer.py`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_view_local_defect_composer_tests.ps1`. All v1 threshold variants passed relation pre-render and constructor preflight and rendered, but selector `threshold_escalation_v1_selector.json` returned `NO_PROGRESS_SELECTED`: palette and side-edge variants improved raw/positive metrics without reducing defect count, while hot-mass/combined variants introduced a new protected `right_three_quarter` palette defect. The side-edge operator was strengthened twice. v2 raised side edge density to `0.045481` and min raw by `+0.020169` but still did not clear the weak-detail defect; selector `threshold_escalation_v2_side_edge_selector.json` returned `NO_PROGRESS_SELECTED`. v3 raised side edge density to `0.048221`, min raw by `+0.027185`, and min positive taste by `+0.027013`, but still did not clear the defect and triggered protected-view hot-mass/taste regressions, so selector `threshold_escalation_v3_side_edge_selector.json` also returned `NO_PROGRESS_SELECTED`. L001 remains `PARTIAL`.

Additional 2026-05-17 scene-authoring intelligence pivot evidence: `tools/model_scene_projection_attribution_advisor.py` was added as a control-plane brain operator for the exact failure mode the user identified: the system must stop making local micro-adjustments when rendered metrics improve but the visible scene is still wrong. It consumes a multiview art-critic report plus `model_scene_screen_space_admission.py` attribution and diagnoses when projected pixels are dominated by low-contrast detail fields rather than meaningful silhouette-bearing scene structure. `tools/model_scene_authoring_intelligence.py` now emits an `attribute_projected_failure` strategy stage and routes projected-detail near-miss plateaus to this advisor. Focused validation passed: `python -m py_compile tools\model_scene_projection_attribution_advisor.py tools\model_scene_authoring_intelligence.py`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_projection_attribution_advisor_tests.ps1`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_authoring_strategy_tests.ps1`; and `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_authoring_intelligence_tests.ps1`. Real rain attribution report `artifacts\scene_authoring_controller\scene_authoring_intelligence_brain_pivot\rain_restage_continuation_v1\projection_attribution_advisor_v1.json` diagnosed `MACRO_STRUCTURE_REQUIRED`: side edge density was `0.037081 < 0.055`, projected detail-field area was `0.454554`, silhouette-bearing area was only `0.009545`, and low-contrast material detail area was `0.633152`. All generated macro-structure variants passed spatial validation, relation pre-render, and constructor preflight. Rendered multiview candidates `macro_side_silhouette_structure`, `side_depth_occlusion_frame`, `macro_silhouette_palette_guard`, and `coverage_budget_arch_frame` did not reduce total defects, so selector `projection_attribution_advisor_v2_selector.json` returned `NO_PROGRESS_SELECTED`. However, this is materially new evidence: `macro_side_silhouette_structure` improved min raw by `+0.006963` and min positive taste by `+0.009498` without protected-view regression, while `coverage_budget_arch_frame` raised the side view score to `0.660806` and side edge density to `0.042831` but still failed the `0.055` weak-detail threshold and regressed `right_three_quarter`. The current limiter is therefore not lack of primitive count; it is insufficient projected, high-contrast, semantically meaningful structure under protected-view coupling. L001 remains `PARTIAL`.

Additional 2026-05-17 measured projection-budget and coupling evidence: `tools/model_scene_projection_aware_variant_generator.py` was strengthened so support-surface search proposals become explicit top/face attachments instead of loose sampled props, and it now has a `light_edge` material preset for high-contrast projected edge-budget experiments. Focused validation passed: `python -m py_compile tools\model_scene_projection_aware_variant_generator.py`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_projection_aware_variant_generator_tests.ps1`. Real projection searches found rear-wall and floor placements that the projection model predicted as visible in `side_profile_contact` and nearly invisible in protected `right_three_quarter`, `table_contact_low`, and `high_contact_check`. The generated rear `top_16` and floor `top_12` variants passed spatial validation, relation pre-render, and constructor preflight, then rendered as `model_authored_multiview_projection_budget_side_rear_top_16_v2.json` and `model_authored_multiview_projection_budget_side_floor_top_12_v1.json`. Selector `projection_budget_rear_floor_selector.json` returned `NO_PROGRESS_SELECTED`: rear `top_16` improved side score to `0.660674` but side edge density only reached `0.039481`, and floor `top_12` regressed side score to `0.648999`. A post-render attribution report for rear `top_16` showed projected `projection_aware_detail` area `0.050984`, yet the rendered edge metric barely moved. This proves a new limiter: object-space projection budgets overestimate effectiveness when z-buffer visibility, material response, and actual image gradients do not match the simple projection model.

Additional 2026-05-17 hot-mass coupling evidence: A more aggressive `model_scene_center_hot_luma_reducer.py` pass from the accepted rain `balanced` checkpoint changed 16 projected contributors and passed spatial, relation pre-render, and constructor preflight gates. Rendering `model_authored_multiview_left_hot_luma_reducer_v2.json` cleared `left_three_quarter` completely and improved mean raw score from `0.732584` to `0.740482` and mean positive taste from `0.815634` to `0.839906`, but selector `left_hot_luma_reducer_v2_selector.json` returned `NO_PROGRESS_SELECTED` because total defect count stayed `3`, `hero` gained a weak-detail defect in addition to palette, and `side_profile_contact` regressed below the min-score tolerance. A composed palette follow-up `left_hot_plus_palette_v1` also rendered but was not admissible: it failed to fix hero palette/detail and reintroduced a left composition defect. This is useful brain evidence: the system can now isolate and clear a single causal rendered defect, but admission requires multi-view coupled repair rather than promoting the local win.

Additional 2026-05-17 rendered-gradient scene-authoring intelligence evidence: `tools/model_scene_rendered_gradient_attribution.py` was added as the next control-plane perception layer. It projects real scene objects into a validation camera, samples the actual rendered image under each projected box, ignores validation-only/non-renderable constructor scaffolding by default, and reports which roles/materials are large-but-flat, real edge contributors, hot-mass contributors, or tiny detail that cannot carry macro structure. `tools/model_scene_authoring_intelligence.py` now accepts a rendered-gradient report as blackboard evidence, scores rendered-gradient deficits into the projected-detail/global-art/protected-view routes, and routes projected-detail near-miss plateaus to rendered-gradient attribution before more macro/micro variants. Focused validation passed: `python -m py_compile tools\model_scene_rendered_gradient_attribution.py tools\model_scene_authoring_intelligence.py`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_rendered_gradient_attribution_tests.ps1`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_authoring_strategy_tests.ps1`; and `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_authoring_intelligence_tests.ps1`. Real rain evidence was generated at `artifacts\scene_authoring_controller\scene_authoring_intelligence_brain_pivot\rain_restage_continuation_v1\side_profile_rendered_gradient_attribution_v1.json` and `projection_budget_side_rear_top_16_rendered_gradient_attribution_v1.json`. The accepted balanced side view has global rendered edge density `0.017736`, far below the `0.055` target, even though existing floor/detail objects have local high-gradient patches. The projection-budget rear `top_16` candidate only raises global edge density to `0.018343`; its `projection_aware_detail` role has mean local edge density `0.056955`, but `flat_area_ratio=0.685500` and `hot_area_ratio=0.921033`, explaining why object-space projected area did not translate into an admissible art improvement. This is not final art, but it is the first explicit brain mechanism for detecting when geometry is technically visible yet visually ineffective.

Additional 2026-05-17 rendered-gradient policy-planner evidence: `tools/model_scene_rendered_gradient_policy_planner.py` was added to turn rendered-gradient attribution into a reusable authoring decision before another render loop. It compares accepted-baseline and rejected-candidate attribution, records policy rejections, emits route hints, and identifies the next admissible action family. `tools/model_scene_authoring_intelligence.py` now accepts `--rendered-gradient-policy-report`, places the policy summary on the blackboard, scores route hints, and uses `execute_rendered_gradient_policy_plan` for projected-detail deficits when a repeated visual policy has already been rejected. Additional 2026-05-17 scene-authoring brain pivot evidence: `tools/model_scene_authoring_brain.py` was added as the architecture-level runbook generator above the specialist tools. It consumes seed, rendered multiview, selector, rendered-gradient, policy, model-critic, and model-proposal-execution reports and emits typed work orders across perception, neural/model advising, bounded proposal execution, validation, rendered admission, and learning/outcome-memory phases. The neural contract is explicit: pretrained/VLM/3D scene models may emit typed relation, support/contact, asset-upgrade, material/light, or camera proposals, but direct scene mutation and completion claims are forbidden; every proposal must pass `model_scene_model_proposal_ingestor.py`, deterministic gates, rendered multiview, and selector admission. Focused validation passed: `python -m py_compile tools\model_scene_authoring_brain.py tools\model_scene_model_guided_repair_loop.py`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_authoring_brain_tests.ps1`; and `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_model_guided_repair_loop_tests.ps1`. A real rain brain artifact was generated at `artifacts\scene_authoring_controller\scene_authoring_intelligence_brain_pivot\rain_restage_continuation_v1\scene_authoring_brain_coupled_left_hot_gradient_v1.json`; it correctly selected `REQUEST_NEURAL_OR_DETERMINISTIC_SCENE_ADVISOR` instead of another manual scene tweak. The deterministic model-advisor boundary then ran on the real coupled rain packet with two rendered images and admitted 16 proposals at `scene_authoring_brain_coupled_left_hot_gradient_v1\model_critic_runner.json`. `tools/model_scene_model_guided_repair_loop.py` was strengthened so rendered palette/material failures can create bounded material-light proposals, not only detail/camera proposals. The real v2 loop at `scene_authoring_brain_coupled_left_hot_gradient_v1\model_guided_repair_loop_v2.json` admitted 18 proposals but produced `MODEL_GUIDED_REPAIR_LOOP_NO_SAFE_VARIANTS`: 16 helper/backer support-contact proposals had no safe bounded edit, one visibility proposal and one material-light proposal were blocked by prior rendered outcome memory. The regenerated brain report `scene_authoring_brain_with_execution_gap_v1.json` now enters `EXPAND_PROPOSAL_EXECUTION_BRIDGE`, which is the correct architectural stopping position: the system needs a stronger proposal-to-executor bridge and/or external scene-model proposal source, not more hand-authored object nudges. L001 remains `PARTIAL`.

Additional 2026-05-17 executable scene-authoring brain evidence: `tools/model_scene_authoring_brain_runtime.py` now turns the neural/advisor boundary into an executable control-plane cycle. It builds a scene-understanding packet, writes a model request containing object ids, relation types, the allowed proposal schema, blocked signatures, and non-mutation instructions, then runs either a deterministic provider or an external-command provider whose only allowed output is proposal JSON. The runtime validates that JSON through `model_scene_model_proposal_ingestor.py`, feeds admitted proposals into `model_scene_model_guided_repair_loop.py`, emits render/selector commands only for preflight-gated bounded variants, and writes an `authoring_training_row.jsonl` so later local learned models can be trained to rank proposal families from packet features plus rendered outcome labels. `tools/model_scene_authoring_brain.py` now includes a `W035_run_scene_authoring_brain_runtime` work order so the runbook can call this executable loop instead of stopping at a passive model request. Additional 2026-05-17 scene-authoring intelligence goal pivot evidence: `tools/model_scene_authoring_outcome_memory.py` was added so the brain can learn from rendered selector failures instead of looping through hundreds of local tweaks. Additional 2026-05-17 scene-authoring intelligence goal evidence: `tools/model_scene_authoring_feedback_compiler.py` was added as the missing feedback-to-brain adapter. Additional 2026-05-17 real feedback-runtime evidence: the feedback compiler was exercised on the real rain checkpoint rather than only fixtures. L001 remains `PARTIAL`; the next capability must be richer generated/retrieved mesh carriers, stronger segmentation/depth/ranking, or a learned/rendered-effect ranker that can choose scene-scale structure likely to clear named defects rather than merely improve scalar metrics.

Additional 2026-05-18 external scene-model provider evidence: `tools/model_scene_external_scene_model_provider.py` now makes `BRAIN-W022` executable. It reads the runtime model request via args or `CORTEX_SCENE_MODEL_REQUEST`/`CORTEX_SCENE_PACKET`/`CORTEX_SCENE_PROPOSALS_OUT`, optionally invokes or sanitizes an external model JSON response, rejects unknown object ids, unsupported actions, duplicate signatures, and completion claims, and emits only typed proposals for the existing ingestor/executor path. When no model response is available it uses a local evidence-conditioned fallback over multiview defects, diagnostic reports, relation graph objects, and policy-ranker state; that fallback exists only to keep the provider boundary testable without credentials. Focused validation passed: `python -m py_compile tools\model_scene_external_scene_model_provider.py tools\model_scene_authoring_brain_runtime.py tools\model_scene_scene_authoring_intelligence_provider.py`; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_external_scene_model_provider_tests.ps1`; and `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_authoring_brain_runtime_tests.ps1`. The focused provider test proves fallback proposals are admitted by `model_scene_model_proposal_ingestor.py` and unsafe model-response proposals are rejected. Real runtime `scene_authoring_brain_runtime_scene_intelligence_v39_external_provider_adapter\scene_authoring_brain_runtime.json` exercised the adapter through `--provider external_command --external-command "python tools/model_scene_external_scene_model_provider.py"` against the rain checkpoint with six rendered images, the failed multiview report, perception diagnostics, outcome memory, projection-effect memory, policy-ranker report, and compiled feedback. It admitted 2 proposals and produced 2 render-eligible variants. Both rendered through six-view validation, but selector `scene_authoring_brain_runtime_scene_intelligence_v39_external_provider_adapter\bounded_execution\rendered_progress_selector.json` returned `NO_PROGRESS_SELECTED`: both candidates kept 2 art-critic defects, with the vertical fixture min raw delta `-0.000751` and the low crossbar at `0.0`. This is negative but useful evidence: the executable external-provider architecture is now present, but the local fallback is not a substitute for a real pretrained/VLM/depth scene model capable of proposing semantically correct multi-object edits.

Additional 2026-05-19 Scene Authoring Intelligence V1 evidence: `tools/model_scene_authoring_intelligence_v1.py` now adds the first brief-to-plan control slice above the local repair loops. It converts high-level scene briefs into selected family plans, enclosed room-shell intent, zones, macro slots, focal objects, supporting props, lighting/material/camera intents, semantic scene-graph nodes and relations, forbidden model behaviors, asset-catalog expansion gaps for bathroom/bedroom/workshop/store/street, and full-scene batch work orders. Focused validation passed: `python -m py_compile tools\model_scene_authoring_intelligence_v1.py` and `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_model_scene_authoring_intelligence_v1_tests.ps1`, which verified three briefs, three families, nine candidate work orders, required V1 capability coverage, advisory-only model boundary, relation/environment/render/rank work-order gates, and `NOT_L001_COMPLETE` completion policy. A durable seven-family run produced `artifacts\scene_authoring_intelligence_v1\controller_report.json` with status `SCENE_AUTHORING_INTELLIGENCE_V1_CONTROLLER_READY`, `7` briefs, `7` selected families (`home_kitchen_lantern`, `home_office_evening`, `school_classroom_day`, `basketball_gym_day`, `neon_streamer_concert`, `red_light_room`, `stadium_night_match`), and `28` full-scene candidate work orders. This is the correct architectural direction because it moves generation upstream from object-level micro-polish to scene brief, semantic plan, graph contract, candidate batch, validation, and ranking.

Additional 2026-05-19 V1 loop-runner evidence: `tools/model_scene_authoring_intelligence_v1_loop.py` now wires generated semantic-plan seeds into the reusable generate -> understand -> critique -> repair -> rank architecture. For each seed it runs spatial membership, scene-local environment validation, relation pre-render gate, constructor preflight, model-facing critique packet export, `model_scene_authoring_intelligence.py` route planning, and `model_scene_neural_authoring_orchestrator.py` repair work-order generation. Additional 2026-05-19 macro-director evidence: `tools/model_scene_authoring_director.py` was added as a non-mutating control-plane planner that reads rendered multiview evidence plus selector reports and explicitly detects local-operator saturation. For office v49 it flags weak desktop/shelf context, zero naturalistic assets, and mean edge density `0.077993`. Additional 2026-05-24 asset-enrichment admission evidence: `tools/run_scene_authoring_asset_enrichment_repair_batch.py` now preserves canonical readable categories when a mesh replacement hides an implementation-role source object. Additional 2026-05-24 frontier-audit evidence: `tools/run_scene_authoring_full_pipeline.py` now treats optional asset enrichment as a new measurable visual frontier instead of a blind side path. After asset-enrichment promotion and integrity, it writes an optional visual-audit source report, runs `run_scene_authoring_visual_scene_audit_batch.py` on the enriched promoted set, then runs a second bounded `visual_detail_lod` repair/render/admission/integrity loop against that enriched frontier. Additional 2026-05-24 transactional support-remap and material-guard evidence: the v6b kitchen rejection exposed a deeper reusable support-ownership defect. The tabletop details were supported by a hidden source table after asset enrichment, while the visible table was a replacement mesh; naive grouping either left details detached from the visible support or flattened the warm/cool place-setting palette into one material. `tools/run_scene_authoring_semantic_detail_group_repair_batch.py` now remaps only members of actually applied semantic groups from hidden supports to visible replacements, records `hidden_support_child_remap` provenance, and skips semantic groups when material diversity would be flattened by a single-material generated mesh. The focused regression test now proves hidden-support group members are moved to the visible replacement table, unrelated detail is preserved, and replacement meshes are emitted only for admitted groups. Validation passed with `python -m py_compile tools\run_scene_authoring_semantic_detail_group_repair_batch.py` and `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_scene_authoring_semantic_detail_group_repair_batch_tests.ps1`. Targeted v7e evidence: `python tools\run_scene_authoring_semantic_detail_group_repair_batch.py --visual-audit-report artifacts\scene_authoring_kernel_v1\frontier_positive_detail_lod_probe_from_v15_visual_audit_v2_semantic_detail_critic\visual_scene_audit_batch_report.json --out-root artifacts\scene_authoring_kernel_v1\semantic_detail_group_probe_from_v17_selected_v7e_transactional_remap --scene-id stress_rainy_galley_kitchen --scene-id stress_late_night_study --max-scenes 0` produced `SEMANTIC_DETAIL_GROUP_REPAIR_BATCH_READY`, `candidate_ready_scene_count=2`, `added_object_count=7`, `hidden_member_count=37`, and zero physical/semantic failures; the kitchen tabletop group was explicitly skipped with `reason=material_diversity_would_be_flattened`. Rendered admission selected both scenes with `render_failed_scene_count=0` and `rejected_scene_count=0`; promotion reported `promoted_count=2`; integrity reported `SCENE_AUTHORING_INTEGRITY_BATCH_PASS`, `pass_scene_count=2`, `blocking_check_count=0`; and review-packet export produced 12 images. The final visual audit reports `VISUAL_SCENE_AUDIT_NEEDS_WORK`, `issue_count=3`: office now passes with `VISUAL_SCENE_AUDIT_PASS`, `primitive_ratio=0.47059`, `thin_primitive_count=9`, `detail_like_count=15`, and positive taste `PASS`, while kitchen remains needs-work for positive taste, thin primitive fragmentation, and detail fragmentation. L001 remains `PARTIAL`: this is a stronger reusable scene-graph repair/admission mechanism and admits both kitchen and office safely, but kitchen still needs further taste/detail work and the broader family set is not accepted by the user.

### CE-FA-038

1. Requirement ID: CE-FA-038
2. Requirement text: Add a pretrained-model final-art source boundary so TRELLIS/Hunyuan-style generators may propose assets without directly mutating Cortex scenes or bypassing renderer contracts.

What currently exists: Release validation exists; final-art scripts exist and pass; repo hygiene and SOTA guardrails pass after preserving the required ledger exception and future-content guardrail; visual-probe runtime budget validation retries whole runtime captures for transient timing spikes while keeping the existing 16.7 ms budget threshold unchanged.

9. Exact proof: Completion Gate command block in this ledger.

Latest evidence: Initial 2026-05-14 failures showed non-deterministic established-scene visual-probe GPU spikes, including `rt_showcase_hero_release gpu_frame_ms=28.232704` in `build/bin/logs/runs/release_validation_20260514_114519_203_35316_70107ef1/release_validation_summary.json` and up to `60.868608` in focused `visual_probe_validation_20260514_120422_752_97532_10ef2d31`. Comparison with single-case and regular smoke runs showed normal `rt_showcase` GPU times around 2-4 ms, while the failing focused report attributed the outlier mostly to `ShadowPass` at about 29 ms, indicating a transient runtime timing spike rather than a deterministic renderer, shader, or final-art content regression. `tools/run_visual_baseline_contract_tests.ps1` now retries each runtime case up to three whole engine captures and accepts only a capture that satisfies the original exact metrics. Focused validation passed with `powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_visual_baseline_contract_tests.ps1 -RuntimeSmoke -MaxRuntimeCases 12 -NoBuild` in `build/bin/logs/runs/visual_baseline_contract_20260514_121411_452_74372_ef067f14`; visual probe passed with `powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_visual_probe_validation.ps1 -NoBuild` in `build/bin/logs/runs/visual_probe_validation_20260514_121509_026_86456_5f9ff8fa`; full release validation passed with `powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\run_release_validation.ps1` in `build/bin/logs/runs/release_validation_20260514_121631_051_97456_aa3809e2`.
